# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

+ BorrowRecord::isOverdue
+ Library::addDocument
+ Library::registerMember
+ Member::borrowBook
+ Member::extendReturnDueDate
+ Member::listBorrowedBookTitles
+ Member::returnBook

6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Read the requirement and the class diagram.
2. For each operation specification, determine whether it is complete and consistent with the requirement.
3. If there is any error, correct the erroneous specification.
4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Library::addDocument
Summary: Adds a document to the library's collection.
Algorithm: 
  1. Add the provided doc object to the documents list.
Related Features: documents
Related Classes: Document
Input: doc (Document): The document instance to be added.
Output: void
Pre-condition: doc is not null.
Post-condition: The library's documents list includes the new document.

Library::registerMember
Summary: Registers a new member in the library.
Algorithm: 
  1. Validate firstName and surname are not null, empty, or whitespace.
  2. Check if any member in members list has (firstName + surname) equal to input.
  3. If valid and unique, create a Member, add to members list, return true.
  4. Otherwise, return false.
Related Features: members
Related Classes: Member
Input: firstName (String), surname (String)
Output: boolean: True if registration was successful, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: firstName and surname must not be null/empty/whitespace.
Post-condition: If true, a new Member is added to the members list.

Algorithm:
  1. Find the BorrowRecord associated with the given book title.
  2. If the record exists and today is >= borrowingDate and today < returnDue:

# Operation Specifications
BorrowRecord::isOverdue
Summary: Checks if the current borrow record is overdue relative to a given date.
Algorithm: 
  1. If today > returnDue, return true.
  2. Else, return false.
Related Features: returnDue
Related Classes: None
Input: today (Date): The reference date to check against.
Output: boolean: True if today is past the returnDue date, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: Returns true if the record is overdue, false otherwise.

BorrowRecord::extendDueDate
Summary: Extends the return due date of a borrow record by 7 days.
Algorithm: 
  1. Calculate newDate = current returnDue + 7 days.
  2. Update returnDue = newDate.
  3. Return newDate.
Related Features: returnDue
Related Classes: None
Input: today (Date): The current date (used for validation logic in the caller).
Output: Date: The updated return due date.
Pre-condition: The operation is called only when the extension criteria are met.
Post-condition: The returnDue attribute is updated to exactly 7 days after the previous value.

Related Features: returnDue
Related Classes: None
Input: today (Date): The reference date to check against.
Output: boolean: True if today is past the returnDue date, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: Returns true if the record is overdue, false otherwise.

BorrowRecord::extendDueDate
Summary: Extends the return due date of a borrow record by 7 days.
Algorithm: 
  1. Calculate newDate = current returnDue + 7 days.
  2. Update returnDue = newDate.
  3. Return newDate.
Related Features: returnDue
Related Classes: None
Input: today (Date): The current date (used for validation logic in the caller).
Output: Date: The updated return due date.
Pre-condition: The operation is called only when the extension criteria are met.
Post-condition: The returnDue attribute is updated to exactly 7 days after the previous value.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Operation Specifications
Member::borrowBook
Summary: A member borrows a book by title and creates a borrow record.
Algorithm:
  1. Retrieve the document from the library by title.
  2. Check if the document exists, is an instance of Book, is not currently borrowed, and the member has fewer than 3 borrow records.
  3. If criteria met, create a new BorrowRecord with today's date and a due date (today + 7 days), add to borrowRecords, and return true.
  4. Otherwise, return false.
Related Features: borrowRecords, library
Related Classes: Book, BorrowRecord, Document, Library
Input: bookTitle (String), today (Date)
Output: boolean: True if borrowing succeeded, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: today is a valid date.
Post-condition: If true, a new BorrowRecord is added to the member's borrowRecords.

Member::returnBook
Summary: Returns a borrowed book to the library.
Algorithm:
  1. Find the BorrowRecord for the given bookTitle.
  2. If no such record exists, return false.
  3. If BorrowRecord::isOverdue(today) is true, return false.
  4. Otherwise, remove the BorrowRecord from borrowRecords and return true.
Related Features: borrowRecords
Related Classes: BorrowRecord
Input: bookTitle (String), today (Date)
Output: boolean: True if the book was successfully returned, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: today is a valid date.
Post-condition: The BorrowRecord is removed from the member's borrowRecords if the return is successful.

Member::listBorrowedBookTitles
Summary: Lists the titles of all books currently borrowed by the member.
Algorithm:
  1. Iterate through the borrowRecords list.
  2. Collect the title from the associated book in each BorrowRecord.
  3. Return the list of titles (empty if no records).
Related Features: borrowRecords
Related Classes: BorrowRecord, Book
Input: None
Output: List<String>: A list of borrowed book titles.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: Return a list containing the titles of borrowed books.

Member::extendReturnDueDate
Summary: Extends the return due date of a borrowed book by seven days.
Algorithm:
  1. Find the BorrowRecord associated with the given book title.
  2. If the record exists and today is >= borrowingDate and today < returnDue:
     a. Call BorrowRecord::extendDueDate(today) to move the due date forward by 7 days.
     b. Return the new due date.
  3. Otherwise, return the current due date (unchanged).
Related Features: borrowRecords
Related Classes: BorrowRecord
Input: bookTitle (String), today (Date)
Output: Date: The updated due date or the original due date.
Pre-condition: The member must have a borrow record for the specified book title.
Post-condition: The BorrowRecord's returnDue date is updated if the conditions are met.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information

  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.

  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;

4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: A member borrows a book by title and creates a borrow record.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Retrieve the document from the library by title.
   *   2. Check if the document exists, is an instance of Book, is not currently borrowed by any member, and the member has fewer than 3 borrow records.
   *   3. If criteria met, create a new BorrowRecord with today's date and a due date (today + 7 days), add to borrowRecords, and return true.
   *   4. Otherwise, return false.
   * Related Features: borrowRecords, library
   * Related Classes: Book, BorrowRecord, Document, Library
   * Input: bookTitle (String), today (Date)
   * Output: boolean: True if borrowing succeeded, false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: today is a valid date.
   * Post-condition: If true, a new BorrowRecord is added to the member's borrowRecords.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model todayDataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean borrowBook(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    if (getLibrary() == null || getBorrowRecords().size() >= 3) {
      return false;
    }

    Document targetDoc = null;
    for (Document doc : getLibrary().getDocuments()) {
      if (doc.getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
        targetDoc = doc;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (!(targetDoc instanceof Book)) {
      return false;
    }

    // Check if borrowed by any member
    for (Member member : getLibrary().getMembers()) {
      for (BorrowRecord record : member.getBorrowRecords()) {
        if (record.getBook() != null && record.getBook().getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
          return false;
        }
      }
    }

    // Create record
    BorrowRecord newRecord = MunicipalLibraryFactory.eINSTANCE.createBorrowRecord();
    newRecord.setBook((Book) targetDoc);
    newRecord.setBorrowingDate(today);

    Calendar cal = Calendar.getInstance();
    cal.setTime(today);
    cal.add(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR, 7);
    newRecord.setReturnDue(cal.getTime());

    getBorrowRecords().add(newRecord);
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Returns a borrowed book to the library.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Find the BorrowRecord for the given bookTitle.
   *   2. If no such record exists, return false.
   *   3. If BorrowRecord::isOverdue(today) is true, return false.

  public boolean returnBook(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    BorrowRecord targetRecord = null;
    for (BorrowRecord record : getBorrowRecords()) {
      if (record.getBook() != null && record.getBook().getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
        targetRecord = record;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (targetRecord == null) {
      return false;
    }

    if (targetRecord.isOverdue(today)) {
      return false;
    }

    getBorrowRecords().remove(targetRecord);
    return true;
  }

   * Summary: Extends the return due date of a borrowed book by seven days.

  public Date extendReturnDueDate(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    BorrowRecord targetRecord = null;
    for (BorrowRecord record : getBorrowRecords()) {
      if (record.getBook() != null && record.getBook().getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
        targetRecord = record;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (targetRecord != null) {
      if (!today.before(targetRecord.getBorrowingDate()) && today.before(targetRecord.getReturnDue())) {

      }
      return targetRecord.getReturnDue();
    }
    return null;
  }

        return eInternalContainer().eInverseRemove(this, MunicipalLibraryPackage.LIBRARY__MEMBERS, Library.class, msgs);
    }
    return super.eBasicRemoveFromContainerFeature(msgs);
  }

  public Object eGet(int featureID, boolean resolve, boolean coreType)
  {
    switch (featureID)
    {
      case MunicipalLibraryPackage.MEMBER__LIBRARY:
        return getLibrary();
      case MunicipalLibraryPackage.MEMBER__FIRST_NAME:
        return getFirstName();
      case MunicipalLibraryPackage.MEMBER__SURNAME:
        return getSurname();
      case MunicipalLibraryPackage.MEMBER__BORROW_RECORDS:
        return getBorrowRecords();
    }
    return super.eGet(featureID, resolve, coreType);
  }

  @Override
  public void eSet(int featureID, Object newValue)
  {

    {
      case MunicipalLibraryPackage.MEMBER__LIBRARY:
        setLibrary((Library)newValue);
        return;
      case MunicipalLibraryPackage.MEMBER__FIRST_NAME:
        setFirstName((String)newValue);
        return;
      case MunicipalLibraryPackage.MEMBER__SURNAME:

        return;

      case MunicipalLibraryPackage.MEMBER__SURNAME:
        return SURNAME_EDEFAULT == null ? surname != null : !SURNAME_EDEFAULT.equals(surname);
      case MunicipalLibraryPackage.MEMBER__BORROW_RECORDS:
        return borrowRecords != null && !borrowRecords.isEmpty();
    }
    return super.eIsSet(featureID);
  }

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

+ BorrowRecord::isOverdue
+ Library::addDocument
+ Library::registerMember
+ Member::borrowBook
+ Member::extendReturnDueDate
+ Member::listBorrowedBookTitles
+ Member::returnBook

Post-condition: If successful, a new BorrowRecord is added to borrowRecords., org.eclipse.emf.ecore.impl.EOperationImpl@45494046 (name: extendReturnDueDate) (ordered: true, unique: true, lowerBound: 0, upperBound: 1)=Summary: Extends the due date of a borrowed book by 7 days.
Algorithm: 
  Find record in borrowRecords where record.book.title == bookTitle.
  If found and today >= record.borrowingDate and today < record.returnDue:
    record.extendDueDate(today), return record.returnDue.
  Else: return current record.returnDue (or null if not found).
Related Features: borrowRecords
Related Classes: BorrowRecord
Input: bookTitle (String), today (Date)
Output: Date - the updated returnDue date or the original date.

  Find record in borrowRecords matching bookTitle.
  If not found, return false.
  If record.isOverdue(today), return false.
  Else, remove record from borrowRecords, return true.
Related Features: borrowRecords
Related Classes: BorrowRecord

2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;

5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;

8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

3. If there is any error, correct the erroneous specification.
4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
BorrowRecord::extendDueDate
Summary: Moves the current returnDue date forward by seven days.
Algorithm: returnDue = returnDue + 7 days.
Related Features: returnDue
Related Classes: None
Input: today (Date) - the current system date.
Output: The new returnDue date (Date).
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: returnDue is updated to the date 7 days after the original returnDue.

BorrowRecord::isOverdue
Summary: Checks if the current system date is past the returnDue date.
Algorithm: return today > returnDue ? true : false
Related Features: returnDue
Related Classes: None
Input: today (Date) - the current system date.
Output: boolean - true if today is after returnDue, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: None.

# Operation Specifications
Library::registerMember
Summary: Registers a new member if the full name is unique and valid.
Algorithm: 
  If firstName or surname is null/empty/whitespace, return false.
  If any member in members has same firstName and surname, return false.
  Else, create new Member, add to members, return true.
Related Features: members
Related Classes: Member
Input: firstName (String), surname (String)
Output: boolean - true if registration succeeds, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: firstName and surname are provided.
Post-condition: If successful, a new Member is added to the library's members list.

Library::addDocument
Summary: Adds a document instance to the library's collection.
Algorithm: documents.add(doc)
Related Features: documents
Related Classes: Document
Input: doc (Document) - the document object to be added.
Output: None.
Pre-condition: doc is not null.
Post-condition: The library's documents list contains the new document.

Algorithm: documents.add(doc)
Related Features: documents
Related Classes: Document
Input: doc (Document) - the document object to be added.
Output: None.
Pre-condition: doc is not null.
Post-condition: The library's documents list contains the new document.

5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;

8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Operation Specifications
Member::listBorrowedBookTitles
Summary: Lists the titles of all currently borrowed books for the member.
Algorithm:
  Iterate through borrowRecords.
  Collect and return the titles of the books associated with each record.
Related Features: borrowRecords
Related Classes: BorrowRecord, Book
Input: None
Output: List<String> - a list of borrowed book titles.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: None.

Member::borrowBook
Summary: A member borrows a document by title and creates a borrow record.
Algorithm:
  Find the Document in library.documents with the given title.
  If not found, or not a Book, or book is already borrowed (check all members' borrowRecords), or member already holds 3 books, return false.
  Else, create a new BorrowRecord with borrowingDate = today, returnDue = today + 7 days.
  Add the record to borrowRecords and return true.
Related Features: borrowRecords, library
Related Classes: Document, Book, BorrowRecord, Library
Input: bookTitle (String), today (Date)
Output: boolean - true if borrowing succeeds, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: today is a valid date format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Post-condition: If successful, a new BorrowRecord is added to member's borrowRecords.

Member::returnBook
Summary: A member returns a borrowed book by title.
Algorithm:
  Find the BorrowRecord in borrowRecords where record.book.title == bookTitle.
  If not found, return false.
  If record.isOverdue(today), return false.
  Else, remove the record from borrowRecords and return true.
Related Features: borrowRecords
Related Classes: BorrowRecord
Input: bookTitle (String), today (Date)
Output: boolean - true if return is accepted, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: today is a valid date format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Post-condition: If successful, the borrow record is removed from the member's list.

Member::extendReturnDueDate
Summary: Extends the return due date of a borrowed book.
Algorithm:
  Find the BorrowRecord in borrowRecords where record.book.title == bookTitle.
  If not found, return current due date (or handle as error).
  If today >= borrowRecord.borrowingDate AND today < borrowRecord.returnDue:
    Set borrowRecord.returnDue = borrowRecord.returnDue + 7 days.
  Return the (updated or original) returnDue date.
Related Features: borrowRecords
Related Classes: BorrowRecord
Input: bookTitle (String), today (Date)
Output: Date - the new return due date.
Pre-condition: today is a valid date format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Post-condition: The returnDue date of the specified BorrowRecord is updated if the condition is met.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

   * Summary: A member borrows a book by title and creates a borrow record.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Retrieve the library instance.
   *   2. Find the Document in library.documents with the given title.
   *   3. If not found, or not a Book, or book is already borrowed by any member, or current number of borrowRecords >= 3, return false.
   *   4. Else, create a new BorrowRecord with borrowingDate = today, returnDue = today + 7 days.
   *   5. Add the record to borrowRecords and return true.

  public boolean borrowBook(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {

    Library lib = getLibrary();
    if (lib == null) return false;
    
    Book targetBook = null;
    for (Document doc : lib.getDocuments()) {
      if (doc instanceof Book && doc.getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
        targetBook = (Book) doc;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (targetBook == null) return false;
    
    for (Member m : lib.getMembers()) {
      for (BorrowRecord br : m.getBorrowRecords()) {
        if (br.getBook() == targetBook) return false;
      }

    record.setBorrowingDate(today);

    record.setReturnDue(cal.getTime());

  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.
